Research pipeline

The agent researches; the target evaluates. Official scores are computed only on the target side.

Agent side · AWSautonomous research
1Reference videoone human demonstration
2AI agentplans, writes code, analyzes failures
3Training simulatorbuilt and tuned by the agent
4Policy learningtraining and local evaluation
submit policy→
Target side · LECAR-S2black box
5Target evaluationexecutes the policy · official score from three cameras
↑7 · Improvementnext hypothesis → AI agent
6 · Research feedback ←
scorescamera videosjoint logsfailure diagnosisexperiment history
↓Official resultstored after every rollout

Live agents

One autonomous agent per task, 100 target submissions each. “Live” marks facts read from target records at export time.

Agent's diagnosis

The exact S21 acquisition and grouped reversal transfer, but the loaded upward stroke is weaker than the four new fitted models predict. The flight is small and subsequent hand contacts do not retain the ball. Reject as an improvement; keep diagnostic evidence only.

worked: Target-verified suspended acquisition remains reproducible. Grouped stroke causes a hardware-compliant upward release. Intended80msreverse lowers the ball before firing, as predicted.

failed: No20cm toss or fulltask improvement. Ball recontacts at3.56–3.57s and3.62–3.77s, then leaves and reaches environment near4.02s. Late5splus contact/ball measurements are unknown, not false or a hold.

evidence: Official failure.10[.10,.10], hardwarecompliant maxspeedratio.8681, valid5000steps, inference.086/.187ms. Release3.38s/flight.18s/apex.014824m, consistent arc. All13feedbackhashes and allfourfinite5000x20logarrays verified; launch/contact/late grids from allthreeviews inspected. Prefix vsS21 before3.2s cameraRMSE.181mm, jointRMSE2.925mrad. Own fourfittedmodel fullQ0 tosses3.71–4.10cm overpredict official1.48cm; olderprimary~2.2cm andverysoftno-flight bracket uncertainty.

limitation: Contact is inferred from geometry. Own arc/rotation/energy estimates are not privileged state or measured target forces. Official confidence.455 because lateballleavesview; scorebounds remain exactly.10. Target and local contact laws remain unvalidated globally.

Moves are decoded from the puzzle-state track of the official sensor report (the state the agent also receives, relative to the back-left-bottom corner, so turning the whole cube never counts as a layer turn; L, D and B turns appear as R′, U′ and F′). A valid turn is a change between two observed settled states that is exactly one layer turn; several turns without a settled state between them, state changes not explained by up to three turns, motion after the cube was lost and hidden motion are uncertain, never counted as valid.
